171. DEFINITION OF DONE ДЛЯ MVP
MVP считается готовым не тогда, когда:
«Приложение запускается».
А когда новый пользователь может:
1.
Установить приложение.
2.
Авторизоваться.
3.
Получить бесплатную первую генерацию.
4.
Выбрать человека.
5.
Выбрать повод.
6.
Получить персональные рекомендации.
7.
Выбрать проверенный шаблон.
8.
Загрузить фото.
9.
Добавить личную информацию.
10.
Получить качественное видео.
11.
Посмотреть.
12.
Скачать.
13.
Отправить другу.
14.
Друг открыть видео.
15.
Поставить реакцию.
16.
Пользователь увидеть статистику.
17.
Следующее поздравление оплатить через ЮKassa.
18.
Администратор увидеть всю цепочку.

172. И САМОЕ ГЛАВНОЕ — КАК Я БЫ ОПРЕДЕЛИЛ MVP
Не надо пытаться в первой версии доказать:
«Мы можем всё».
Надо доказать одну вещь:
Человек загрузил свою фотографию → выбрал человека → получил потрясающее персональное поздравление → отправил его другу → друг офигел → захотел ответить.
Если эта цепочка работает — у нас есть продукт.
Всё остальное можно наращивать.

173. ФИНАЛЬНАЯ СТРАТЕГИЯ ПРОДУКТА
Я бы зафиксировал её буквально как внутренний манифест:
Дарагент — это не AI-генератор.
Это персональный режиссёр поздравлений.
Он знает, кого мы поздравляем, кто поздравляет, какие между ними отношения, что любит этот человек, какие у них общие приколы и какой повод.
Он не заставляет пользователя становиться сценаристом.
Он предлагает проверенные идеи и превращает их в короткий фильм, песню или сцену.
Главный результат — не красивое AI-видео.
Главный результат — эмоция получателя.
И именно поэтому я считаю правильным фундаментом:
People Engine → Event Engine → Relationship Engine → Recommendation Engine → Creative Brief → Template Engine → Prompt Compiler → AI Orchestrator → Quality Gate → Share Engine → Analytics → Template Intelligence.
Это и есть архитектурное ядро.
А Android, Telegram Mini App, VK Mini App, сайт — только разные двери в этот движок.

полная PostgreSQL-схема — все таблицы, поля, типы, индексы и связи;
полная структура FastAPI-проекта по папкам и модулям;
OpenAPI/API-контракты всех MVP endpoint'ов с request/response JSON;
точная JSON Schema Creative Brief;
JSON Schema шаблона со сценами, переменными, условиями и версиями;
алгоритм Recommendation Engine;
алгоритм Prompt Compiler;
машина состояний Generation;
очередь и worker architecture;
Yandex Disk Storage Service;
ЮKassa + wallet ledger + webhook flow;
система бонусов;
система Analytics Events;
структура Admin Panel;
полная карта Android-проекта;
конкретный UX каждого экрана;
первые 40 шаблонов с их Creative Brief-полями и сценарными каркасами;
Docker Compose для dev/staging/production;
security и backup strategy;
план разработки по спринтам от пустого репозитория до первого платного пользователя.


пользователь → получатель → бриф → рекомендации → проверенный шаблон → сцена/переменные → AI-generation pipeline → ассеты → модерация → оплата → бонусы → доставка → Telegram/VK → группы → праздники → конкурсы → аналитика → админка.

Готовый SQL-файл:
Скачать daragent_postgresql_schema.sql
Что входит в схему
1. Пользовательская часть
users
user_auth_identities
user_preferences
devices
Поддержаны телефон, email, Telegram, VK и другие способы авторизации.
2. Люди и получатели
recipients
recipient_assets
relationship_types
Получатель хранит не просто имя, а:
отношения с отправителем;
интересы;
черты характера;
любимые вещи;
профессии;
город;
личные факты;
запрещённые темы;
фотографии.
Это важно для главной идеи Дарагента — поздравление должно быть персональным, а не просто подставлять имя.
3. Проект поздравления
Главная сущность:
projects
Один project = одно конкретное поздравление.
Он проходит примерно такой жизненный цикл:
draft → briefing → recommended → awaiting_payment → paid → queued → generating → review → ready → delivered
При этом сохраняются:
получатель;
повод;
выбранная концепция;
шаблон;
цена;
скидки;
оплата;
финальная генерация.
4. Creative Brief
creative_briefs
и
brief_questions
brief_answers
Здесь реализована наша идея интерактивного сбора информации:
кто поздравляет → кого → по какому поводу → какие отношения → какой характер → юмор → эмоции → личная шутка → хобби → история → желаемая фраза → чего избегать.
То есть AI получает структурированный Creative Brief, а не просто длинный пользовательский prompt.
5. Система рекомендаций
recommendations
Позволяет сначала предложить пользователю, например:
Эпичный вариант
Брутальный вариант
Трогательный вариант
Безумно смешной вариант
Киношный вариант
И только после выбора запускать конкретный production pipeline.
Это соответствует нашей идее не генерировать всё с нуля, а использовать заранее протестированные концепции и шаблоны.
6. Шаблоны
Есть полноценная иерархия:
templates
↓
template_versions
↓
scenes
↓
scene_variables
Это позволит иметь, например:
Брутальное поздравление коллеге
→ версия 3
→ сцена 1
→ переменные:
recipient_name
recipient_job
inside_joke
sender_name
hobby
catch_phrase
И менять шаблон без поломки уже созданных поздравлений.
7. Киношные сцены
Для нашей идеи со сценами из кино предусмотрены:
scenes
с полями для:
названия источника;
типа источника;
reference;
статуса прав;
длительности;
исходного файла;
preview.
То есть архитектура позволяет использовать не только обычные шаблоны, но и сюжетные/киношные механики.
8. AI pipeline
Это одна из самых важных частей.
Есть:
generations
↓
generation_steps
↓
generation_jobs
Например одно поздравление может иметь:
Generation
 ├── script
 ├── image
 ├── voice
 ├── music
 ├── lip_sync
 ├── video
 └── final
Каждый этап знает:
provider;
модель;
prompt;
input;
output;
стоимость;
длительность;
ошибку;
количество попыток.
Это позволит менять AI-провайдеров без переделки бизнес-логики Дарагента.
9. AI-провайдеры
providers
Можно зарегистрировать:
LLM;
image generation;
video generation;
voice;
music;
moderation;
storage;
messaging;
payment.
И отдельно:
prompt_templates
То есть prompts становятся данными БД, а не зашитыми строками в Python-коде.
10. Медиа
Есть два уровня:
storage_objects
— физический объект в MinIO/S3.
assets
— бизнес-сущность Дарагента.
Поэтому один и тот же физический файл можно нормально связывать с пользователем, получателем, генерацией и проектом.
Поддерживаются:
фото;
изображения;
видео;
аудио;
голос;
музыка;
thumbnails;
subtitles;
scripts;
исходники.
11. Модерация
moderation_checks
и статус модерации у assets.
Можно проверять:
пользовательские фотографии;
generated content;
финальные видео.
12. Оплата
Есть полноценный финансовый слой:
payments
wallets
wallet_transactions
entitlements
bonus_grants
promo_codes
promo_redemptions
Это позволит реализовать не только оплату одной генерации за ориентиром 500 ₽+, но и впоследствии:
пакеты;
бонусы;
промокоды;
бесплатные генерации;
реферальные бонусы;
возвраты;
внутренний баланс.
Особенно важна таблица:
entitlements
Она позволяет выдать пользователю:
first_generation = 1
при регистрации.
И после использования списать это право, не смешивая бесплатную генерацию с денежным балансом.
13. Доставка
deliveries
delivery_links
share_events
Поддержаны:
Telegram;
VK;
WhatsApp;
ссылка;
скачивание;
email.
Можно отслеживать:
создано → отправляется → отправлено → открыто
14. Группы
Для идеи кругов людей:
groups
group_members
group_recipients
Можно создавать:
Семья
Друзья
Одноклассники
Коллеги
Команда
Свой круг
Это позволит потом сделать очень сильную механику:
«У кого скоро день рождения?»
и автоматически предлагать поздравление.
15. Календарь праздников
Есть:
holidays
holiday_dates
Поддерживаются:
государственные;
профессиональные;
тематические;
персональные;
пользовательские.
То есть впоследствии можно построить ленту:
Сегодня День строителя
Завтра День программиста
Через 5 дней день рождения Сергея
16. Конкурсы
Для идеи соревнования поздравлений:
contests
contest_entries
contest_votes
Например:
«Самое смешное поздравление недели»
или:
«Поздравление класса»
17. Реферальная система
Есть:
referral_codes
referrals
Это уже закладывает возможность:
пригласи друга → получи бонус / бесплатную генерацию.
18. Аналитика
analytics_events
attribution_events
Можно анализировать весь funnel:
install
 ↓
registration
 ↓
recipient_created
 ↓
brief_started
 ↓
brief_completed
 ↓
recommendations_shown
 ↓
template_selected
 ↓
payment_started
 ↓
payment_success
 ↓
generation_started
 ↓
generation_completed
 ↓
video_opened
 ↓
shared
Это очень важно для Дарагента, потому что впоследствии мы должны понимать не только сколько пользователей, а на каком именно этапе они отваливаются.
19. Админка
admin_users
audit_logs
Можно контролировать:
пользователей;
проекты;
генерации;
шаблоны;
сцены;
AI-провайдеров;
платежи;
модерацию;
ошибки.
Главная архитектурная идея
Я бы считал ядром Дарагента вот эту цепочку:
USER
  │
  ├── RECIPIENT
  │
  └── PROJECT
        │
        ├── CREATIVE BRIEF
        │
        ├── RECOMMENDATIONS
        │       │
        │       └── TEMPLATE VERSION
        │               │
        │               ├── SCENES
        │               └── VARIABLES
        │
        ├── PAYMENT / ENTITLEMENT
        │
        └── GENERATION
                │
                ├── SCRIPT
                ├── IMAGE
                ├── VOICE
                ├── MUSIC
                ├── VIDEO
                └── FINAL
                        │
                        └── DELIVERY
                              ├── Telegram
                              ├── VK
                              ├── Link
                              └── Download

Именно эта модель, на мой взгляд, не даст нам загнать MVP в угол.
При этом я сознательно не превращал БД в монстра из сотен таблиц. В схеме уже заложены точки расширения для киношных сцен, песен, рэпа, голосов, AI-персонажей, групп, календаря, конкурсов, рефералов и т. д., но сами дополнительные механики не обязаны быть полностью реализованы в первом релизе.
Важный момент: перед использованием в production я бы следующим шагом сделал отдельную v2-версию схемы с PostgreSQL migration-файлами (001_initial.sql, 002_auth.sql, 003_generation.sql...) + ER-диаграммой + описанием каждой таблицы и каждого поля, потому что для разработки Дарагента это будет гораздо удобнее, чем один монолитный SQL.

-- ============================================================
-- ДАРАГЕНТ / DARAGENT
-- PostgreSQL 16+ — canonical MVP database schema
-- Locale: ru-RU, currency: RUB
-- Generated: 2026-08-17
-- ============================================================

BEGIN;

CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS pgcrypto;
CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS citext;
CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS pg_trgm;

CREATE SCHEMA IF NOT EXISTS daragent;
SET search_path TO daragent, public;

-- ============================================================
-- ENUMS
-- ============================================================

CREATE TYPE user_status AS ENUM ('active','blocked','deleted');
CREATE TYPE auth_provider AS ENUM ('phone','email','telegram','vk','google','apple');
CREATE TYPE project_status AS ENUM (
  'draft','briefing','recommended','awaiting_payment','paid',
  'queued','generating','review','ready','delivered','cancelled','failed'
);
CREATE TYPE project_visibility AS ENUM ('private','unlisted');
CREATE TYPE recipient_status AS ENUM ('draft','active','archived');
CREATE TYPE relationship_type AS ENUM (
  'partner','spouse','parent','child','sibling','relative','friend',
  'colleague','boss','employee','client','teacher','classmate','other'
);
CREATE TYPE brief_status AS ENUM ('draft','completed','locked');
CREATE TYPE mood_type AS ENUM (
  'warm','touching','funny','brutal','epic','romantic','friendly',
  'official','ironic','surprising','nostalgic','festive'
);
CREATE TYPE recommendation_status AS ENUM ('generated','shown','selected','rejected','expired');
CREATE TYPE template_status AS ENUM ('draft','active','archived','disabled');
CREATE TYPE template_kind AS ENUM (
  'video','movie_scene','character','rap','song','voice','greeting_card','gif'
);
CREATE TYPE generation_status AS ENUM (
  'created','queued','processing','review','approved','rejected',
  'completed','failed','cancelled'
);
CREATE TYPE generation_type AS ENUM (
  'script','text','image','voice','music','video','lip_sync',
  'face_animation','composite','final'
);
CREATE TYPE asset_type AS ENUM (
  'photo','image','video','audio','voice','music','thumbnail',
  'subtitle','script','document','source'
);
CREATE TYPE asset_status AS ENUM ('uploaded','processing','ready','failed','deleted');
CREATE TYPE provider_kind AS ENUM (
  'llm','image','video','voice','music','storage','messaging','payment','moderation'
);
CREATE TYPE provider_status AS ENUM ('active','inactive','degraded');
CREATE TYPE job_status AS ENUM ('queued','running','success','failed','cancelled','retrying');
CREATE TYPE payment_status AS ENUM ('pending','authorized','paid','failed','cancelled','refunded','partially_refunded');
CREATE TYPE payment_method AS ENUM ('bank_card','sbp','wallet','promo','bonus');
CREATE TYPE transaction_type AS ENUM (
  'deposit','purchase','refund','bonus_credit','bonus_debit',
  'promo_credit','promo_debit','adjustment'
);
CREATE TYPE transaction_status AS ENUM ('pending','completed','failed','reversed');
CREATE TYPE bonus_reason AS ENUM (
  'registration','first_generation','referral','campaign','birthday',
  'manual','refund','loyalty','achievement'
);
CREATE TYPE delivery_channel AS ENUM ('telegram','vk','whatsapp','link','download','email','other');
CREATE TYPE delivery_status AS ENUM ('created','sending','sent','opened','failed','cancelled');
CREATE TYPE notification_type AS ENUM (
  'generation_started','generation_ready','generation_failed',
  'payment_success','payment_failed','promo','holiday','system'
);
CREATE TYPE notification_channel AS ENUM ('push','telegram','vk','email','sms','in_app');
CREATE TYPE notification_status AS ENUM ('queued','sent','delivered','read','failed');
CREATE TYPE holiday_kind AS ENUM ('state','professional','thematic','personal','custom');
CREATE TYPE holiday_status AS ENUM ('draft','active','archived');
CREATE TYPE group_type AS ENUM ('family','friends','class','work','custom');
CREATE TYPE membership_role AS ENUM ('owner','admin','member');
CREATE TYPE contest_status AS ENUM ('draft','active','finished','cancelled');
CREATE TYPE contest_entry_status AS ENUM ('pending','approved','rejected','winner');
CREATE TYPE promo_type AS ENUM ('percent','fixed','free_generation','bonus');
CREATE TYPE promo_status AS ENUM ('draft','active','paused','expired');
CREATE TYPE moderation_status AS ENUM ('pending','approved','rejected','needs_review');
CREATE TYPE audit_action AS ENUM (
  'create','update','delete','login','logout','payment','refund',
  'generation_start','generation_complete','moderation','admin_action'
);

-- ============================================================
-- COMMON
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE app_settings (
  key              text PRIMARY KEY,
  value_json       jsonb NOT NULL,
  description      text,
  updated_at       timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE TABLE users (
  id               uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  status           user_status NOT NULL DEFAULT 'active',
  display_name     text,
  first_name       text,
  last_name        text,
  email            citext,
  phone            text,
  avatar_asset_id  uuid,
  locale           text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'ru-RU',
  timezone         text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'Europe/Moscow',
  currency         char(3) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'RUB',
  birth_date       date,
  metadata         jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
  created_at       timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at       timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  last_seen_at     timestamptz,
  deleted_at       timestamptz
);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ux_users_email ON users(email) WHERE email IS NOT NULL AND deleted_at IS NULL;
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ux_users_phone ON users(phone) WHERE phone IS NOT NULL AND deleted_at IS NULL;
CREATE INDEX ix_users_status ON users(status);
CREATE INDEX ix_users_created_at ON users(created_at);

CREATE TABLE user_auth_identities (
  id               uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  user_id          uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  provider         auth_provider NOT NULL,
  provider_user_id text NOT NULL,
  email            citext,
  phone            text,
  credentials_json jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
  created_at       timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  last_login_at    timestamptz
);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ux_auth_provider_user ON user_auth_identities(provider, provider_user_id);
CREATE INDEX ix_auth_user ON user_auth_identities(user_id);

CREATE TABLE user_preferences (
  user_id          uuid PRIMARY KEY REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  preferred_moods  jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]'::jsonb,
  preferred_styles jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]'::jsonb,
  notification_settings jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
  marketing_opt_in boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
  analytics_opt_in boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT true,
  updated_at       timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE TABLE devices (
  id               uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  user_id          uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  platform         text NOT NULL,
  app_version      text,
  device_model     text,
  push_token       text,
  metadata         jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
  created_at       timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  last_seen_at     timestamptz
);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ux_devices_push_token ON devices(push_token) WHERE push_token IS NOT NULL;
CREATE INDEX ix_devices_user ON devices(user_id);

-- ============================================================
-- PEOPLE / RECIPIENTS
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE recipients (
  id               uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  owner_user_id    uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  status           recipient_status NOT NULL DEFAULT 'active',
  first_name       text NOT NULL,
  last_name        text,
  nickname         text,
  gender           text,
  birth_date       date,
  city             text,
  occupation       text,
  relationship     relationship_type,
  relationship_label text,
  contact_phone    text,
  contact_email    citext,
  notes            text,
  interests        jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]'::jsonb,
  traits           jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]'::jsonb,
  favorite_things  jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]'::jsonb,
  forbidden_topics jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]'::jsonb,
  metadata         jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
  created_at       timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at       timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  archived_at      timestamptz
);

CREATE INDEX ix_recipients_owner ON recipients(owner_user_id);
CREATE INDEX ix_recipients_owner_name ON recipients(owner_user_id, first_name, last_name);
CREATE INDEX ix_recipients_status ON recipients(status);

CREATE TABLE recipient_assets (
  id               uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  recipient_id     uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES recipients(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  asset_id         uuid NOT NULL,
  is_primary       boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
  sort_order       integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  created_at       timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ux_recipient_asset ON recipient_assets(recipient_id, asset_id);
CREATE INDEX ix_recipient_assets_recipient ON recipient_assets(recipient_id);

CREATE TABLE relationship_types (
  code             relationship_type PRIMARY KEY,
  title            text NOT NULL,
  sort_order       integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  is_active        boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT true
);

-- ============================================================
-- PROJECT / CREATIVE BRIEF
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE projects (
  id               uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  owner_user_id    uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  recipient_id     uuid REFERENCES recipients(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  title            text,
  status           project_status NOT NULL DEFAULT 'draft',
  visibility       project_visibility NOT NULL DEFAULT 'private',
  occasion_code    text,
  occasion_title   text,
  holiday_id       uuid,
  requested_delivery_at timestamptz,
  selected_recommendation_id uuid,
  selected_template_version_id uuid,
  final_generation_id uuid,
  price_rub         numeric(12,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  bonus_discount_rub numeric(12,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  promo_discount_rub numeric(12,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  paid_rub          numeric(12,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  metadata         jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
  created_at       timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at       timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  completed_at     timestamptz,
  cancelled_at     timestamptz
);

CREATE INDEX ix_projects_owner ON projects(owner_user_id);
CREATE INDEX ix_projects_recipient ON projects(recipient_id);
CREATE INDEX ix_projects_status ON projects(status);
CREATE INDEX ix_projects_owner_status ON projects(owner_user_id, status);
CREATE INDEX ix_projects_created_at ON projects(created_at DESC);
CREATE INDEX ix_projects_holiday ON projects(holiday_id);

CREATE TABLE creative_briefs (
  id               uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  project_id       uuid NOT NULL UNIQUE REFERENCES projects(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  status           brief_status NOT NULL DEFAULT 'draft',
  occasion_text    text,
  sender_role      text,
  recipient_role    text,
  relationship     relationship_type,
  relationship_text text,
  desired_mood     mood_type,
  desired_length_sec integer,
  humor_level      smallint CHECK (humor_level BETWEEN 0 AND 100),
  emotion_level    smallint CHECK (emotion_level BETWEEN 0 AND 100),
  surprise_level   smallint CHECK (surprise_level BETWEEN 0 AND 100),
  personalization_level smallint CHECK (personalization_level BETWEEN 0 AND 100),
  inside_joke      text,
  hobbies_text     text,
  character_traits text,
  memorable_story  text,
  desired_phrase  text,
  forbidden_topics text,
  sender_message   text,
  personalization_answers jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
  selected_options jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
  created_at       timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at       timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  completed_at     timestamptz
);

CREATE TABLE brief_questions (
  id               uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  code             text NOT NULL UNIQUE,
  question         text NOT NULL,
  help_text        text,
  answer_type      text NOT NULL,
  options_json     jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]'::jsonb,
  occasion_codes   text[] NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}',
  relationship_types relationship_type[] NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}',
  is_required      boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
  sort_order       integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  is_active        boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT true
);

CREATE INDEX ix_brief_questions_active_order ON brief_questions(is_active, sort_order);

CREATE TABLE brief_answers (
  id               uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  brief_id         uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES creative_briefs(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  question_id      uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES brief_questions(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  answer_text      text,
  answer_json      jsonb,
  created_at       timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at       timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ux_brief_answer ON brief_answers(brief_id, question_id);
CREATE INDEX ix_brief_answers_brief ON brief_answers(brief_id);

CREATE TABLE project_moods (
  project_id       uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES projects(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  mood             mood_type NOT NULL,
  weight           numeric(5,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
  PRIMARY KEY(project_id, mood)
);

-- ============================================================
-- CATALOG: OCCASIONS / HOLIDAYS / TAGS
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE occasions (
  id               uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  code             text NOT NULL UNIQUE,
  title            text NOT NULL,
  description      text,
  icon              text,
  sort_order       integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  is_active        boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT true,
  metadata         jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb
);

CREATE TABLE holidays (
  id               uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  code             text NOT NULL UNIQUE,
  title            text NOT NULL,
  kind             holiday_kind NOT NULL,
  month            smallint CHECK (month BETWEEN 1 AND 12),
  day              smallint CHECK (day BETWEEN 1 AND 31),
  country_code     char(2),
  description      text,
  status           holiday_status NOT NULL DEFAULT 'active',
  metadata         jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb
);

CREATE INDEX ix_holidays_kind_status ON holidays(kind, status);
CREATE INDEX ix_holidays_month_day ON holidays(month, day);

CREATE TABLE holiday_dates (
  id               uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  holiday_id       uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES holidays(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  date             date NOT NULL,
  year             integer NOT NULL,
  is_official      boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
  metadata         jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb
);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ux_holiday_date ON holiday_dates(holiday_id, date);
CREATE INDEX ix_holiday_dates_date ON holiday_dates(date);

CREATE TABLE tags (
  id               uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  code             text NOT NULL UNIQUE,
  title            text NOT NULL,
  category         text,
  is_active        boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT true
);

CREATE TABLE template_tags (
  template_id      uuid NOT NULL,
  tag_id           uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES tags(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  PRIMARY KEY(template_id, tag_id)
);

-- ============================================================
-- TEMPLATES / SCENES / VARIABLES
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE templates (
  id               uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  code             text NOT NULL UNIQUE,
  title            text NOT NULL,
  description      text,
  kind             template_kind NOT NULL,
  status           template_status NOT NULL DEFAULT 'draft',
  category         text,
  occasion_codes   text[] NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}',
  relationship_types relationship_type[] NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}',
  moods            mood_type[] NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}',
  min_price_rub    numeric(12,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  base_price_rub   numeric(12,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  estimated_duration_sec integer,
  difficulty       smallint CHECK (difficulty BETWEEN 1 AND 5),
  personalization_score smallint CHECK (personalization_score BETWEEN 0 AND 100),
  preview_asset_id uuid,
  metadata         jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
  created_at       timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at       timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE INDEX ix_templates_status_kind ON templates(status, kind);
CREATE INDEX ix_templates_category ON templates(category);
CREATE INDEX ix_templates_occasion_codes ON templates USING gin(occasion_codes);
CREATE INDEX ix_templates_moods ON templates USING gin(moods);

CREATE TABLE template_versions (
  id               uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  template_id      uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES templates(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  version          integer NOT NULL,
  status           template_status NOT NULL DEFAULT 'draft',
  schema_version   text NOT NULL DEFAULT '1.0',
  prompt_config    jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
  render_config    jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
  personalization_config jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
  validation_config jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
  max_duration_sec integer,
  created_at       timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  published_at     timestamptz,
  retired_at       timestamptz
);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ux_template_version ON template_versions(template_id, version);
CREATE INDEX ix_template_versions_status ON template_versions(status);

CREATE TABLE scenes (
  id               uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  template_id      uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES templates(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  code             text NOT NULL,
  title            text NOT NULL,
  description      text,
  source_title     text,
  source_type      text,
  source_reference text,
  rights_status    text,
  duration_sec     integer,
  source_asset_id  uuid,
  preview_asset_id uuid,
  scene_config     jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
  created_at       timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ux_scene_template_code ON scenes(template_id, code);
CREATE INDEX ix_scenes_template ON scenes(template_id);
CREATE INDEX ix_scenes_rights_status ON scenes(rights_status);

CREATE TABLE scene_variables (
  id               uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  scene_id         uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES scenes(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  code             text NOT NULL,
  title            text NOT NULL,
  variable_type    text NOT NULL,
  description      text,
  required         boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
  default_value    jsonb,
  validation_json  jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
  options_json     jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]'::jsonb,
  sort_order       integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0
);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ux_scene_variable_code ON scene_variables(scene_id, code);
CREATE INDEX ix_scene_variables_scene ON scene_variables(scene_id);

CREATE TABLE template_variables (
  id               uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  template_version_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES template_versions(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  code             text NOT NULL,
  title            text NOT NULL,
  variable_type    text NOT NULL,
  source_path      text,
  required         boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
  default_value    jsonb,
  validation_json  jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
  sort_order       integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0
);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ux_template_variable_code ON template_variables(template_version_id, code);

-- ============================================================
-- RECOMMENDATIONS
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE recommendations (
  id               uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  project_id       uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES projects(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  template_version_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES template_versions(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  status           recommendation_status NOT NULL DEFAULT 'generated',
  rank             smallint NOT NULL,
  score            numeric(8,4),
  match_reasons    jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]'::jsonb,
  explanation      text,
  generated_by_model text,
  created_at       timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  selected_at      timestamptz
);

CREATE INDEX ix_recommendations_project ON recommendations(project_id, rank);
CREATE INDEX ix_recommendations_status ON recommendations(status);

-- ============================================================
-- ASSETS / STORAGE
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE storage_objects (
  id               uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  bucket           text NOT NULL,
  object_key       text NOT NULL,
  original_name    text,
  mime_type        text,
  size_bytes       bigint,
  sha256           text,
  width            integer,
  height           integer,
  duration_ms      bigint,
  storage_provider text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'minio',
  metadata         jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
  created_at       timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ux_storage_object ON storage_objects(bucket, object_key);
CREATE INDEX ix_storage_sha256 ON storage_objects(sha256);
CREATE INDEX ix_storage_created_at ON storage_objects(created_at DESC);

CREATE TABLE assets (
  id               uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  owner_user_id    uuid REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  type             asset_type NOT NULL,
  status           asset_status NOT NULL DEFAULT 'uploaded',
  storage_object_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES storage_objects(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  title            text,
  description      text,
  duration_sec     numeric(10,3),
  width            integer,
  height           integer,
  mime_type        text,
  checksum        text,
  is_private       boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT true,
  moderation_status moderation_status NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending',
  metadata         jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
  created_at       timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at       timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  deleted_at       timestamptz
);

CREATE INDEX ix_assets_owner ON assets(owner_user_id);
CREATE INDEX ix_assets_type_status ON assets(type, status);
CREATE INDEX ix_assets_moderation ON assets(moderation_status);
CREATE INDEX ix_assets_created_at ON assets(created_at DESC);

ALTER TABLE users
  ADD CONSTRAINT fk_users_avatar_asset
  FOREIGN KEY (avatar_asset_id) REFERENCES assets(id) ON DELETE SET NULL;

ALTER TABLE recipients
  ADD CONSTRAINT fk_recipient_asset_placeholder
  FOREIGN KEY (id) REFERENCES recipients(id) DEFERRABLE INITIALLY DEFERRED;

-- remove the harmless self-FK immediately; it exists only to avoid circular DDL ordering
ALTER TABLE recipients DROP CONSTRAINT fk_recipient_asset_placeholder;

ALTER TABLE recipient_assets
  ADD CONSTRAINT fk_recipient_assets_asset
  FOREIGN KEY (asset_id) REFERENCES assets(id) ON DELETE CASCADE;

ALTER TABLE projects
  ADD CONSTRAINT fk_projects_holiday
  FOREIGN KEY (holiday_id) REFERENCES holidays(id) ON DELETE SET NULL;

ALTER TABLE projects
  ADD CONSTRAINT fk_projects_selected_recommendation
  FOREIGN KEY (selected_recommendation_id) REFERENCES recommendations(id) ON DELETE SET NULL;

ALTER TABLE projects
  ADD CONSTRAINT fk_projects_selected_template
  FOREIGN KEY (selected_template_version_id) REFERENCES template_versions(id) ON DELETE SET NULL;

-- ============================================================
-- PROVIDERS / PROMPTS
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE providers (
  id               uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  code             text NOT NULL UNIQUE,
  name             text NOT NULL,
  kind             provider_kind NOT NULL,
  status           provider_status NOT NULL DEFAULT 'active',
  base_url         text,
  model_name       text,
  capabilities     jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
  pricing_config   jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
  rate_limit_config jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
  config_encrypted text,
  created_at       timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at       timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE INDEX ix_providers_kind_status ON providers(kind, status);

CREATE TABLE prompt_templates (
  id               uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  code             text NOT NULL UNIQUE,
  title            text NOT NULL,
  generation_type  generation_type NOT NULL,
  version          integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
  system_prompt    text,
  user_prompt_template text NOT NULL,
  output_schema    jsonb,
  model_params    jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
  is_active       boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT true,
  created_at      timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at      timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ux_prompt_version ON prompt_templates(code, version);
CREATE INDEX ix_prompt_type_active ON prompt_templates(generation_type, is_active);

-- ============================================================
-- GENERATION PIPELINE
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE generations (
  id               uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  project_id       uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES projects(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  parent_generation_id uuid REFERENCES generations(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  template_version_id uuid REFERENCES template_versions(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  type             generation_type NOT NULL,
  status           generation_status NOT NULL DEFAULT 'created',
  attempt          integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
  requested_by_user_id uuid REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  provider_id      uuid REFERENCES providers(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  model_name       text,
  input_json       jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
  output_json      jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
  error_code       text,
  error_message    text,
  cost_rub         numeric(12,4) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  duration_ms      bigint,
  started_at       timestamptz,
  completed_at     timestamptz,
  created_at       timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE INDEX ix_generations_project ON generations(project_id, created_at);
CREATE INDEX ix_generations_status ON generations(status);
CREATE INDEX ix_generations_parent ON generations(parent_generation_id);
CREATE INDEX ix_generations_type_status ON generations(type, status);

CREATE TABLE generation_steps (
  id               uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  generation_id    uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES generations(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  step_no          integer NOT NULL,
  step_code        text NOT NULL,
  type             generation_type NOT NULL,
  status           generation_status NOT NULL DEFAULT 'created',
  provider_id      uuid REFERENCES providers(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  prompt_template_id uuid REFERENCES prompt_templates(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  input_json       jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
  output_json      jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
  error_code       text,
  error_message    text,
  cost_rub         numeric(12,4) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  duration_ms      bigint,
  started_at       timestamptz,
  completed_at     timestamptz,
  created_at       timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ux_generation_step ON generation_steps(generation_id, step_no);
CREATE INDEX ix_generation_steps_status ON generation_steps(status);
CREATE INDEX ix_generation_steps_type ON generation_steps(type);

CREATE TABLE generation_inputs (
  generation_id    uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES generations(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  asset_id         uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES assets(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  role             text NOT NULL,
  sort_order       integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  metadata         jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
  PRIMARY KEY(generation_id, asset_id, role)
);

CREATE INDEX ix_generation_inputs_asset ON generation_inputs(asset_id);

CREATE TABLE generation_outputs (
  generation_id    uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES generations(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  asset_id         uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES assets(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  role             text NOT NULL,
  sort_order       integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  metadata         jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
  PRIMARY KEY(generation_id, asset_id, role)
);

CREATE INDEX ix_generation_outputs_asset ON generation_outputs(asset_id);

CREATE TABLE generation_variables (
  id               uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  generation_id    uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES generations(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  code             text NOT NULL,
  value_json       jsonb,
  source           text,
  created_at       timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ux_generation_variable ON generation_variables(generation_id, code);

ALTER TABLE projects
  ADD CONSTRAINT fk_projects_final_generation
  FOREIGN KEY (final_generation_id) REFERENCES generations(id) ON DELETE SET NULL;

CREATE TABLE generation_reviews (
  id               uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  generation_id    uuid NOT NULL UNIQUE REFERENCES generations(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  status           moderation_status NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending',
  reviewer_user_id uuid REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  score            numeric(6,3),
  comments         text,
  rejection_reason text,
  created_at       timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  reviewed_at      timestamptz
);

-- ============================================================
-- JOB QUEUE / WEBHOOKS
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE generation_jobs (
  id               uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  generation_id    uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES generations(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  generation_step_id uuid REFERENCES generation_steps(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  queue_name       text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'default',
  status           job_status NOT NULL DEFAULT 'queued',
  priority         integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 100,
  attempts         integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  max_attempts     integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 3,
  available_at     timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  locked_at        timestamptz,
  locked_by        text,
  started_at       timestamptz,
  finished_at      timestamptz,
  last_error      text,
  payload         jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
  created_at      timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE INDEX ix_generation_jobs_pick
  ON generation_jobs(status, priority DESC, available_at, created_at)
  WHERE status IN ('queued','retrying');

CREATE INDEX ix_generation_jobs_generation ON generation_jobs(generation_id);

CREATE TABLE provider_webhooks (
  id               uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  provider_id      uuid REFERENCES providers(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  external_event_id text,
  event_type       text,
  payload          jsonb NOT NULL,
  signature_valid  boolean,
  processed        boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
  processing_error text,
  received_at      timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  processed_at     timestamptz
);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ux_provider_webhook_event
  ON provider_webhooks(provider_id, external_event_id)
  WHERE external_event_id IS NOT NULL;

CREATE INDEX ix_provider_webhooks_unprocessed
  ON provider_webhooks(processed, received_at)
  WHERE processed = false;

-- ============================================================
-- BILLING / WALLET / PROMO / BONUSES
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE pricing_plans (
  id               uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  code             text NOT NULL UNIQUE,
  title            text NOT NULL,
  description      text,
  price_rub        numeric(12,2) NOT NULL,
  included_generations integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
  valid_days       integer,
  is_active        boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT true,
  metadata         jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
  created_at       timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE TABLE product_prices (
  id               uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  template_id      uuid REFERENCES templates(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  pricing_plan_id  uuid REFERENCES pricing_plans(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  price_rub        numeric(12,2) NOT NULL,
  currency         char(3) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'RUB',
  valid_from       timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  valid_to         timestamptz,
  is_active        boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT true
);

CREATE INDEX ix_product_prices_template ON product_prices(template_id, is_active);
CREATE INDEX ix_product_prices_plan ON product_prices(pricing_plan_id, is_active);

CREATE TABLE wallets (
  id               uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  user_id          uuid NOT NULL UNIQUE REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  balance_rub      numeric(14,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (balance_rub >= 0),
  bonus_balance    numeric(14,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (bonus_balance >= 0),
  updated_at       timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE TABLE wallet_transactions (
  id               uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  wallet_id        uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES wallets(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  type             transaction_type NOT NULL,
  status           transaction_status NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending',
  amount_rub       numeric(14,2) NOT NULL,
  balance_after_rub numeric(14,2),
  bonus_amount_rub numeric(14,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  bonus_balance_after_rub numeric(14,2),
  project_id       uuid REFERENCES projects(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  payment_id       uuid,
  promo_code_id    uuid,
  description      text,
  idempotency_key  text,
  metadata         jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
  created_at       timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ux_wallet_idempotency
  ON wallet_transactions(wallet_id, idempotency_key)
  WHERE idempotency_key IS NOT NULL;

CREATE INDEX ix_wallet_transactions_wallet_date
  ON wallet_transactions(wallet_id, created_at DESC);

CREATE INDEX ix_wallet_transactions_project ON wallet_transactions(project_id);

CREATE TABLE bonus_grants (
  id               uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  user_id          uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  amount_rub       numeric(12,2) NOT NULL CHECK (amount_rub > 0),
  reason           bonus_reason NOT NULL,
  expires_at       timestamptz,
  source_reference text,
  metadata         jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
  created_at       timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  used_at          timestamptz
);

CREATE INDEX ix_bonus_grants_user ON bonus_grants(user_id, expires_at);
CREATE INDEX ix_bonus_grants_active ON bonus_grants(user_id, expires_at)
  WHERE used_at IS NULL;

CREATE TABLE promo_codes (
  id               uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  code             citext NOT NULL UNIQUE,
  type             promo_type NOT NULL,
  status           promo_status NOT NULL DEFAULT 'draft',
  value            numeric(12,2),
  max_uses         integer,
  used_count       integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  min_order_rub    numeric(12,2),
  max_discount_rub numeric(12,2),
  starts_at        timestamptz,
  expires_at       timestamptz,
  template_id      uuid REFERENCES templates(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  metadata         jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
  created_at       timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE INDEX ix_promo_status_dates ON promo_codes(status, starts_at, expires_at);

CREATE TABLE promo_redemptions (
  id               uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  promo_code_id    uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES promo_codes(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  user_id          uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  project_id       uuid REFERENCES projects(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  discount_rub     numeric(12,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  created_at       timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ux_promo_user_project ON promo_redemptions(promo_code_id, user_id, project_id);
CREATE INDEX ix_promo_redemptions_user ON promo_redemptions(user_id);

CREATE TABLE payments (
  id               uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  user_id          uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  project_id       uuid REFERENCES projects(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  provider_id      uuid REFERENCES providers(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  status           payment_status NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending',
  method           payment_method NOT NULL,
  amount_rub       numeric(12,2) NOT NULL,
  bonus_amount_rub numeric(12,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  discount_rub     numeric(12,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  external_payment_id text,
  idempotency_key  text,
  receipt_json     jsonb,
  provider_payload jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
  created_at       timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  paid_at          timestamptz,
  refunded_at      timestamptz
);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ux_payment_external_id ON payments(external_payment_id)
  WHERE external_payment_id IS NOT NULL;

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ux_payment_idempotency ON payments(idempotency_key)
  WHERE idempotency_key IS NOT NULL;

CREATE INDEX ix_payments_user_date ON payments(user_id, created_at DESC);
CREATE INDEX ix_payments_project ON payments(project_id);
CREATE INDEX ix_payments_status ON payments(status);

ALTER TABLE wallet_transactions
  ADD CONSTRAINT fk_wallet_tx_payment
  FOREIGN KEY (payment_id) REFERENCES payments(id) ON DELETE SET NULL;

ALTER TABLE wallet_transactions
  ADD CONSTRAINT fk_wallet_tx_promo
  FOREIGN KEY (promo_code_id) REFERENCES promo_codes(id) ON DELETE SET NULL;

-- ============================================================
-- FREE FIRST GENERATION / USER ENTITLEMENTS
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE entitlements (
  id               uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  user_id          uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  code             text NOT NULL,
  quantity         integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 1 CHECK (quantity >= 0),
  consumed         integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (consumed >= 0),
  expires_at       timestamptz,
  source           text,
  source_reference text,
  created_at       timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE INDEX ix_entitlements_user ON entitlements(user_id, code);
CREATE INDEX ix_entitlements_available
  ON entitlements(user_id, code, expires_at)
  WHERE consumed < quantity;

-- ============================================================
-- DELIVERY / SHARING
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE delivery_links (
  id               uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  project_id       uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES projects(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  generation_id    uuid REFERENCES generations(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  token_hash       text NOT NULL UNIQUE,
  expires_at       timestamptz,
  max_views        integer,
  view_count       integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  password_hash    text,
  is_active        boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT true,
  created_at       timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  last_opened_at   timestamptz
);

CREATE INDEX ix_delivery_links_project ON delivery_links(project_id);
CREATE INDEX ix_delivery_links_active ON delivery_links(is_active, expires_at);

CREATE TABLE deliveries (
  id               uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  project_id       uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES projects(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  generation_id    uuid REFERENCES generations(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  user_id          uuid REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  channel          delivery_channel NOT NULL,
  status           delivery_status NOT NULL DEFAULT 'created',
  destination      text,
  delivery_link_id uuid REFERENCES delivery_links(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  external_message_id text,
  payload          jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
  created_at       timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  sent_at          timestamptz,
  opened_at        timestamptz,
  failed_at        timestamptz,
  error_message    text
);

CREATE INDEX ix_deliveries_project ON deliveries(project_id, created_at DESC);
CREATE INDEX ix_deliveries_status ON deliveries(status);
CREATE INDEX ix_deliveries_channel ON deliveries(channel);

CREATE TABLE share_events (
  id               uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  project_id       uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES projects(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  generation_id    uuid REFERENCES generations(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  user_id          uuid REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  channel          delivery_channel NOT NULL,
  referral_code    text,
  metadata         jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
  created_at       timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE INDEX ix_share_events_project ON share_events(project_id, created_at DESC);
CREATE INDEX ix_share_events_channel ON share_events(channel);
CREATE INDEX ix_share_events_referral ON share_events(referral_code);

-- ============================================================
-- GROUPS / CIRCLES
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE groups (
  id               uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  owner_user_id    uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  type             group_type NOT NULL DEFAULT 'custom',
  name             text NOT NULL,
  description      text,
  avatar_asset_id  uuid REFERENCES assets(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  metadata         jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
  created_at       timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at       timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE INDEX ix_groups_owner ON groups(owner_user_id);

CREATE TABLE group_members (
  group_id         uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES groups(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  user_id          uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  role             membership_role NOT NULL DEFAULT 'member',
  joined_at        timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  PRIMARY KEY(group_id, user_id)
);

CREATE INDEX ix_group_members_user ON group_members(user_id);

CREATE TABLE group_recipients (
  group_id         uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES groups(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  recipient_id     uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES recipients(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  added_by_user_id uuid REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  created_at       timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  PRIMARY KEY(group_id, recipient_id)
);

CREATE INDEX ix_group_recipients_recipient ON group_recipients(recipient_id);

-- ============================================================
-- CONTESTS / SOCIAL MECHANICS
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE contests (
  id               uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  title            text NOT NULL,
  description      text,
  status           contest_status NOT NULL DEFAULT 'draft',
  starts_at        timestamptz,
  ends_at          timestamptz,
  rules_json       jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
  prizes_json      jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]'::jsonb,
  created_by_user_id uuid REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  created_at       timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE INDEX ix_contests_status_dates ON contests(status, starts_at, ends_at);

CREATE TABLE contest_entries (
  id               uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  contest_id       uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES contests(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  project_id       uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES projects(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  user_id          uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  status           contest_entry_status NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending',
  votes_count      integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  score            numeric(10,4),
  metadata         jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
  created_at       timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ux_contest_project ON contest_entries(contest_id, project_id);
CREATE INDEX ix_contest_entries_contest_score ON contest_entries(contest_id, score DESC);
CREATE INDEX ix_contest_entries_user ON contest_entries(user_id);

CREATE TABLE contest_votes (
  id               uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  contest_entry_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES contest_entries(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  user_id          uuid REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  anonymous_token_hash text,
  created_at       timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ux_contest_vote_user
  ON contest_votes(contest_entry_id, user_id)
  WHERE user_id IS NOT NULL;

CREATE INDEX ix_contest_votes_entry ON contest_votes(contest_entry_id);

-- ============================================================
-- NOTIFICATIONS
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE notifications (
  id               uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  user_id          uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  type             notification_type NOT NULL,
  channel          notification_channel NOT NULL,
  status           notification_status NOT NULL DEFAULT 'queued',
  title            text NOT NULL,
  body             text,
  payload          jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
  scheduled_at     timestamptz,
  sent_at          timestamptz,
  read_at          timestamptz,
  created_at       timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE INDEX ix_notifications_user_unread
  ON notifications(user_id, created_at DESC)
  WHERE read_at IS NULL;

CREATE INDEX ix_notifications_queue
  ON notifications(status, scheduled_at)
  WHERE status = 'queued';

-- ============================================================
-- REFERRALS / CAMPAIGNS
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE referral_codes (
  id               uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  user_id          uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  code             text NOT NULL UNIQUE,
  reward_rub       numeric(12,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  reward_generations integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  max_uses         integer,
  used_count       integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  expires_at       timestamptz,
  created_at       timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE INDEX ix_referral_codes_user ON referral_codes(user_id);

CREATE TABLE referrals (
  id               uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  referral_code_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES referral_codes(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  referrer_user_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  referred_user_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  status           text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'registered',
  reward_granted  boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
  created_at       timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ux_referral_referred_user ON referrals(referred_user_id);
CREATE INDEX ix_referrals_referrer ON referrals(referrer_user_id);

CREATE TABLE campaigns (
  id               uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  code             text NOT NULL UNIQUE,
  title            text NOT NULL,
  description      text,
  starts_at        timestamptz,
  ends_at          timestamptz,
  config_json      jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
  is_active        boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false,
  created_at       timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

-- ============================================================
-- ANALYTICS
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE analytics_events (
  id               bigserial PRIMARY KEY,
  user_id          uuid REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  project_id       uuid REFERENCES projects(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  session_id       text,
  event_name       text NOT NULL,
  event_version    integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
  platform         text,
  app_version      text,
  anonymous_id     text,
  properties       jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
  occurred_at      timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE INDEX ix_analytics_event_name_date ON analytics_events(event_name, occurred_at DESC);
CREATE INDEX ix_analytics_user_date ON analytics_events(user_id, occurred_at DESC);
CREATE INDEX ix_analytics_project_date ON analytics_events(project_id, occurred_at DESC);
CREATE INDEX ix_analytics_properties_gin ON analytics_events USING gin(properties);

CREATE TABLE attribution_events (
  id               bigserial PRIMARY KEY,
  user_id          uuid REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  anonymous_id     text,
  source           text,
  medium           text,
  campaign         text,
  content          text,
  referrer         text,
  landing_page     text,
  created_at       timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE INDEX ix_attribution_user ON attribution_events(user_id, created_at DESC);
CREATE INDEX ix_attribution_campaign ON attribution_events(campaign, created_at DESC);

-- ============================================================
-- MODERATION / SAFETY
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE moderation_checks (
  id               uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  asset_id         uuid REFERENCES assets(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  project_id       uuid REFERENCES projects(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  generation_id    uuid REFERENCES generations(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  provider_id      uuid REFERENCES providers(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  status           moderation_status NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending',
  category_scores  jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
  decision_reason  text,
  raw_result      jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
  created_at       timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  completed_at     timestamptz
);

CREATE INDEX ix_moderation_asset ON moderation_checks(asset_id);
CREATE INDEX ix_moderation_project ON moderation_checks(project_id);
CREATE INDEX ix_moderation_generation ON moderation_checks(generation_id);
CREATE INDEX ix_moderation_status ON moderation_checks(status);

-- ============================================================
-- ADMIN / AUDIT
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE admin_users (
  id               uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  user_id          uuid NOT NULL UNIQUE REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  role             text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'admin',
  permissions      jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
  is_active        boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT true,
  created_at       timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  last_login_at    timestamptz
);

CREATE TABLE audit_logs (
  id               bigserial PRIMARY KEY,
  actor_user_id    uuid REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  admin_user_id    uuid REFERENCES admin_users(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  action           audit_action NOT NULL,
  entity_type      text NOT NULL,
  entity_id        uuid,
  before_json      jsonb,
  after_json       jsonb,
  ip_address       inet,
  user_agent       text,
  created_at       timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);

CREATE INDEX ix_audit_entity ON audit_logs(entity_type, entity_id, created_at DESC);
CREATE INDEX ix_audit_actor ON audit_logs(actor_user_id, created_at DESC);
CREATE INDEX ix_audit_created_at ON audit_logs(created_at DESC);

-- ============================================================
-- IDEMPOTENCY / API REQUESTS
-- ============================================================

CREATE TABLE idempotency_keys (
  id               uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  user_id          uuid REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  key              text NOT NULL,
  endpoint         text NOT NULL,
  request_hash     text,
  response_status  integer,
  response_json    jsonb,
  created_at       timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  expires_at       timestamptz
);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ux_idempotency_user_key ON idempotency_keys(user_id, key, endpoint);
CREATE INDEX ix_idempotency_expires ON idempotency_keys(expires_at);

-- ============================================================
-- FOREIGN KEYS THAT REQUIRE LATE BINDING
-- ============================================================

ALTER TABLE projects
  ADD CONSTRAINT fk_projects_selected_recommendation_late
  FOREIGN KEY (selected_recommendation_id)
  REFERENCES recommendations(id) ON DELETE SET NULL;

ALTER TABLE projects
  DROP CONSTRAINT fk_projects_selected_recommendation;

-- ============================================================
-- TRIGGERS: updated_at
-- ============================================================

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION set_updated_at()
RETURNS trigger
LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $$
BEGIN
  NEW.updated_at = now();
  RETURN NEW;
END;
$$;

CREATE TRIGGER trg_users_updated_at
BEFORE UPDATE ON users FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION set_updated_at();

CREATE TRIGGER trg_user_preferences_updated_at
BEFORE UPDATE ON user_preferences FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION set_updated_at();

CREATE TRIGGER trg_recipients_updated_at
BEFORE UPDATE ON recipients FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION set_updated_at();

CREATE TRIGGER trg_projects_updated_at
BEFORE UPDATE ON projects FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION set_updated_at();

CREATE TRIGGER trg_creative_briefs_updated_at
BEFORE UPDATE ON creative_briefs FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION set_updated_at();

CREATE TRIGGER trg_brief_answers_updated_at
BEFORE UPDATE ON brief_answers FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION set_updated_at();

CREATE TRIGGER trg_assets_updated_at
BEFORE UPDATE ON assets FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION set_updated_at();

CREATE TRIGGER trg_templates_updated_at
BEFORE UPDATE ON templates FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION set_updated_at();

CREATE TRIGGER trg_providers_updated_at
BEFORE UPDATE ON providers FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION set_updated_at();

CREATE TRIGGER trg_prompt_templates_updated_at
BEFORE UPDATE ON prompt_templates FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION set_updated_at();

CREATE TRIGGER trg_groups_updated_at
BEFORE UPDATE ON groups FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION set_updated_at();

-- ============================================================
-- BUSINESS INTEGRITY
-- ============================================================

ALTER TABLE creative_briefs
  ADD CONSTRAINT ck_brief_duration
  CHECK (desired_length_sec IS NULL OR desired_length_sec BETWEEN 3 AND 300);

ALTER TABLE templates
  ADD CONSTRAINT ck_template_price
  CHECK (min_price_rub >= 0 AND base_price_rub >= 0);

ALTER TABLE payments
  ADD CONSTRAINT ck_payment_amount
  CHECK (amount_rub >= 0 AND bonus_amount_rub >= 0 AND discount_rub >= 0);

ALTER TABLE wallet_transactions
  ADD CONSTRAINT ck_wallet_transaction_amount
  CHECK (amount_rub <> 0 OR bonus_amount_rub <> 0);

ALTER TABLE contest_entries
  ADD CONSTRAINT ck_contest_votes_nonnegative
  CHECK (votes_count >= 0);

-- ============================================================
-- SEED: BASIC RELATIONSHIPS
-- ============================================================

INSERT INTO relationship_types(code,title,sort_order) VALUES
('partner','Партнёр',10),
('spouse','Супруг/супруга',20),
('parent','Родитель',30),
('child','Ребёнок',40),
('sibling','Брат/сестра',50),
('relative','Родственник',60),
('friend','Друг/подруга',70),
('colleague','Коллега',80),
('boss','Руководитель',90),
('employee','Сотрудник',100),
('client','Клиент',110),
('teacher','Учитель/преподаватель',120),
('classmate','Одноклассник/одноклассница',130),
('other','Другое',999)
ON CONFLICT (code) DO NOTHING;

INSERT INTO app_settings(key,value_json,description) VALUES
('registration.free_generation', '{"enabled": true, "quantity": 1}'::jsonb, 'Первая бесплатная генерация после регистрации'),
('product.default_price', '{"amount_rub": 500}'::jsonb, 'Ориентир минимальной цены генерации'),
('product.default_currency', '"RUB"'::jsonb, 'Валюта MVP'),
('product.default_locale', '"ru-RU"'::jsonb, 'Язык MVP')
ON CONFLICT (key) DO NOTHING;

-- ============================================================
-- COMMENTS / DOMAIN MAP
-- ============================================================

COMMENT ON TABLE users IS 'Пользователи Дарагента';
COMMENT ON TABLE projects IS 'Одна задача/поздравление от идеи до готового результата';
COMMENT ON TABLE recipients IS 'Получатели поздравлений';
COMMENT ON TABLE creative_briefs IS 'Креативный бриф: кто, кого, повод, отношения, настроение, факты и личные детали';
COMMENT ON TABLE recommendations IS '3–5 предварительно подобранных концепций поздравления';
COMMENT ON TABLE templates IS 'Проверенные креативные шаблоны, предпочтительные для MVP';
COMMENT ON TABLE template_versions IS 'Версии шаблонов с конкретными prompt/render конфигурациями';
COMMENT ON TABLE scenes IS 'Сцены, в том числе киношные/сюжетные, используемые шаблонами';
COMMENT ON TABLE scene_variables IS 'Переменные персонализации конкретной сцены';
COMMENT ON TABLE generations IS 'Высокоуровневые AI/рендер-генерации';
COMMENT ON TABLE generation_steps IS 'Шаги пайплайна одной генерации';
COMMENT ON TABLE assets IS 'Фото, видео, аудио, музыка, голос, превью и итоговые файлы';
COMMENT ON TABLE generation_jobs IS 'Асинхронная очередь генерации';
COMMENT ON TABLE payments IS 'Платежи за поздравления/пакеты';
COMMENT ON TABLE wallets IS 'Денежный и бонусный баланс пользователя';
COMMENT ON TABLE entitlements IS 'Право на генерацию: бесплатная, промо, пакетная и т.п.';
COMMENT ON TABLE deliveries IS 'Факт отправки результата через Telegram/VK/ссылку/скачивание и т.п.';
COMMENT ON TABLE holidays IS 'Российские, профессиональные и тематические праздники';
COMMENT ON TABLE groups IS 'Круги людей: семья, друзья, класс, работа и т.п.';
COMMENT ON TABLE contests IS 'Соревнования/конкурсы поздравлений';
COMMENT ON TABLE analytics_events IS 'Продуктовая аналитика';
COMMENT ON TABLE moderation_checks IS 'Модерация пользовательского и AI-контента';
COMMENT ON TABLE audit_logs IS 'Аудит административных и критических действий';

COMMIT;








Для Дарагента я бы строил FastAPI **не как один `main.py` с роутерами**, а как модульный backend, где бизнес-логика поздравлений, AI-generation pipeline, платежи, пользователи и каталог шаблонов разделены.
Оптимальная структура для MVP с запасом на масштабирование:
